+void simulateBodies(Bodies& bodies, const int numSteps) {
+    const std::ptrdiff_t n = static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(bodies.size());
+    Vec3* const positions = bodies.positions.data();
+    Vec3* const velocities = bodies.velocities.data();
+
+    // Very small chunks lose more time at the two step barriers than they
+    // gain from another worker.  Respect the user's OpenMP limit while
+    // avoiding oversubscription on machines with large default teams.
+    constexpr std::ptrdiff_t MIN_BODIES_PER_THREAD = 64;
+    const std::ptrdiff_t usefulThreads =
+        n > 0 ? (n + MIN_BODIES_PER_THREAD - 1) / MIN_BODIES_PER_THREAD : 1;
+    const int maxThreads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    const int teamSize = usefulThreads < maxThreads
+        ? static_cast<int>(usefulThreads)
+        : maxThreads;
 
-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+    // Keep a single team alive for all steps.  Both implicit barriers are
+    // required: all force reads must precede integration, and every position
+    // update must complete before the next step begins.
+#pragma omp parallel num_threads(teamSize) default(none) \
+    shared(positions, velocities) firstprivate(n, numSteps)
+    {
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (std::ptrdiff_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+                const double x = positions[i].x;
+                const double y = positions[i].y;
+                const double z = positions[i].z;
+                double forceX = 0.0;
+                double forceY = 0.0;
+                double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+                for (std::ptrdiff_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+                    const double dx = positions[j].x - x;
+                    const double dy = positions[j].y - y;
+                    const double dz = positions[j].z - z;
+                    const double distSqr =
+                        dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                    const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                    const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                    forceX += dx * invDist3;
+                    forceY += dy * invDist3;
+                    forceZ += dz * invDist3;
+                }
+

+                velocities[i].y += DT * forceY;
+                velocities[i].z += DT * forceZ;
+            }
+
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (std::ptrdiff_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {

+void simulateBodies(Bodies& bodies, const int numSteps) {
+    const std::ptrdiff_t n = static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(bodies.size());
+    Vec3* const positions = bodies.positions.data();
+    Vec3* const velocities = bodies.velocities.data();
+
+    // Very small chunks lose more time at the two step barriers than they
+    // gain from another worker.  Respect the user's OpenMP limit while
+    // avoiding oversubscription on machines with large default teams.
+    constexpr std::ptrdiff_t MIN_BODIES_PER_THREAD = 64;
+    const std::ptrdiff_t usefulThreads =
+        n > 0 ? (n + MIN_BODIES_PER_THREAD - 1) / MIN_BODIES_PER_THREAD : 1;
+    const int maxThreads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    const int teamSize = usefulThreads < maxThreads
+        ? static_cast<int>(usefulThreads)
+        : maxThreads;
 
-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+    // Keep a single team alive for all steps.  Both implicit barriers are
+    // required: all force reads must precede integration, and every position
+    // update must complete before the next step begins.
+#pragma omp parallel num_threads(teamSize) default(none) \
+    shared(positions, velocities) firstprivate(n, numSteps)
+    {
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (std::ptrdiff_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+                const double x = positions[i].x;
+                const double y = positions[i].y;
+                const double z = positions[i].z;
+                double forceX = 0.0;
+                double forceY = 0.0;
+                double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+                for (std::ptrdiff_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+                    const double dx = positions[j].x - x;
+                    const double dy = positions[j].y - y;
+                    const double dz = positions[j].z - z;
+                    const double distSqr =
+                        dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                    const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                    const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                    forceX += dx * invDist3;
+                    forceY += dy * invDist3;
+                    forceZ += dz * invDist3;
+                }
+
+                velocities[i].x += DT * forceX;
+                velocities[i].y += DT * forceY;
+                velocities[i].z += DT * forceZ;
+            }
+
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (std::ptrdiff_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+                positions[i].x += velocities[i].x * DT;
+                positions[i].y += velocities[i].y * DT;
+                positions[i].z += velocities[i].z * DT;
+            }
+        }
     }
 }

+void simulateBodies(Bodies& bodies, const int numSteps) {

+                    const double distSqr =
+                        dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                    const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);

+                    forceY += dy * invDist3;

codex
The split hot-record layout still perturbs exact hashes after repeated steps, despite matching printed statistics. I’m therefore keeping the original `Body` memory layout in the final version and confining optimization to race-free OpenMP worksharing, persistent-team reuse, static scheduling, and adaptive team sizing—the path that can satisfy byte-for-byte result equivalence.

+void randomizeBodies(Bodies& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {
+        // Retain the original per-body random-number ordering.
+        bodies.items[i].pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
+        bodies.items[i].pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;

-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+void simulateBodies(Bodies& bodies, const int numSteps) {
+    const std::ptrdiff_t n = static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(bodies.size());
+    Body* const data = bodies.items.data();
+
+    // Very small chunks lose more time at the two step barriers than they
+    // gain from another worker.  Respect the user's OpenMP limit while
+    // avoiding oversubscription on machines with large default teams.
+    constexpr std::ptrdiff_t MIN_BODIES_PER_THREAD = 64;
+    const std::ptrdiff_t usefulThreads =
+        n > 0 ? (n + MIN_BODIES_PER_THREAD - 1) / MIN_BODIES_PER_THREAD : 1;
+    const int maxThreads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    const int teamSize = usefulThreads < maxThreads
+        ? static_cast<int>(usefulThreads)
+        : maxThreads;
+
+    // Keep a single team alive for all steps.  Both implicit barriers are
+    // required: all force reads must precede integration, and every position
+    // update must complete before the next step begins.
+#pragma omp parallel num_threads(teamSize) default(none) \
+    shared(data) firstprivate(n, numSteps)
+    {
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (std::ptrdiff_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+                const double x = data[i].pos.x;
+                const double y = data[i].pos.y;
+                const double z = data[i].pos.z;
+                double forceX = 0.0;
+                double forceY = 0.0;
+                double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+                for (std::ptrdiff_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+                    const double dx = data[j].pos.x - x;
+                    const double dy = data[j].pos.y - y;
+                    const double dz = data[j].pos.z - z;
+                    const double distSqr =
+                        dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                    const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                    const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                    forceX += dx * invDist3;
+                    forceY += dy * invDist3;
+                    forceZ += dz * invDist3;
+                }
+
+                data[i].vel.x += DT * forceX;
+                data[i].vel.y += DT * forceY;
+                data[i].vel.z += DT * forceZ;
+            }
+
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (std::ptrdiff_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+                data[i].pos.x += data[i].vel.x * DT;
+                data[i].pos.y += data[i].vel.y * DT;
+                data[i].pos.z += data[i].vel.z * DT;
+            }
+        }
     }
 }

+        bodies.items[i].pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
+        bodies.items[i].pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
+        bodies.items[i].pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
+        bodies.items[i].vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
+        bodies.items[i].vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;

-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+void simulateBodies(Bodies& bodies, const int numSteps) {
+    const std::ptrdiff_t n = static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(bodies.size());
+    Body* const data = bodies.items.data();
+
+    // Very small chunks lose more time at the two step barriers than they
+    // gain from another worker.  Respect the user's OpenMP limit while
+    // avoiding oversubscription on machines with large default teams.
+    constexpr std::ptrdiff_t MIN_BODIES_PER_THREAD = 64;
+    const std::ptrdiff_t usefulThreads =
+        n > 0 ? (n + MIN_BODIES_PER_THREAD - 1) / MIN_BODIES_PER_THREAD : 1;
+    const int maxThreads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    const int teamSize = usefulThreads < maxThreads
+        ? static_cast<int>(usefulThreads)
+        : maxThreads;
+
+    // Keep a single team alive for all steps.  Both implicit barriers are
+    // required: all force reads must precede integration, and every position
+    // update must complete before the next step begins.
+#pragma omp parallel num_threads(teamSize) default(none) \
+    shared(data) firstprivate(n, numSteps)
+    {
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (std::ptrdiff_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+                const double x = data[i].pos.x;
+                const double y = data[i].pos.y;
+                const double z = data[i].pos.z;
+                double forceX = 0.0;
+                double forceY = 0.0;
+                double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+                for (std::ptrdiff_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+                    const double dx = data[j].pos.x - x;
+                    const double dy = data[j].pos.y - y;
+                    const double dz = data[j].pos.z - z;
+                    const double distSqr =
+                        dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                    const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                    const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                    forceX += dx * invDist3;
+                    forceY += dy * invDist3;
+                    forceZ += dz * invDist3;
+                }
+
+                data[i].vel.x += DT * forceX;
+                data[i].vel.y += DT * forceY;
+                data[i].vel.z += DT * forceZ;
+            }
+
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (std::ptrdiff_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+                data[i].pos.x += data[i].vel.x * DT;
+                data[i].pos.y += data[i].vel.y * DT;
+                data[i].pos.z += data[i].vel.z * DT;
+            }
+        }
     }
 }

codex
The exact-layout version now matches the untouched serial hash byte-for-byte for small, irregular, medium, and default workloads, independent of the requested thread count. It still reduces the default case from 77 ms serial to roughly 7–10 ms, so this is the stronger final balance: exact semantics plus scalable OpenMP speedup.

-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {

+void simulateBodies(Bodies& bodies, const int numSteps) {
+    const std::ptrdiff_t n = static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(bodies.size());
+    Body* const data = bodies.items.data();
+
+    // Very small chunks lose more time at the two step barriers than they
+    // gain from another worker.  Respect the user's OpenMP limit while
+    // avoiding oversubscription on machines with large default teams.
+    constexpr std::ptrdiff_t MIN_BODIES_PER_THREAD = 64;
+    const std::ptrdiff_t usefulThreads =
+        n > 0 ? (n + MIN_BODIES_PER_THREAD - 1) / MIN_BODIES_PER_THREAD : 1;
+    const int maxThreads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    const int teamSize = usefulThreads < maxThreads
+        ? static_cast<int>(usefulThreads)
+        : maxThreads;
+
+    // Keep a single team alive for all steps.  Both implicit barriers are
+    // required: all force reads must precede integration, and every position
+    // update must complete before the next step begins.
+#pragma omp parallel num_threads(teamSize) default(none) \
+    shared(data) firstprivate(n, numSteps)
+    {
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (std::ptrdiff_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+                const double x = data[i].pos.x;
+                const double y = data[i].pos.y;
+                const double z = data[i].pos.z;
+                double forceX = 0.0;
+                double forceY = 0.0;
+                double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+                for (std::ptrdiff_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+                    const double dx = data[j].pos.x - x;
+                    const double dy = data[j].pos.y - y;
+                    const double dz = data[j].pos.z - z;
+                    const double distSqr =
+                        dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                    const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                    const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                    forceX += dx * invDist3;
+                    forceY += dy * invDist3;
+                    forceZ += dz * invDist3;
+                }
+
+                data[i].vel.x += DT * forceX;
+                data[i].vel.y += DT * forceY;
+                data[i].vel.z += DT * forceZ;
+            }
+
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (std::ptrdiff_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+                data[i].pos.x += data[i].vel.x * DT;
+                data[i].pos.y += data[i].vel.y * DT;
+                data[i].pos.z += data[i].vel.z * DT;
+            }
+        }
     }
 }

    47	void simulateBodies(Bodies& bodies, const int numSteps) {
    48	    const std::ptrdiff_t n = static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(bodies.size());
    49	    Body* const data = bodies.items.data();
    50	
    51	    // Very small chunks lose more time at the two step barriers than they
    52	    // gain from another worker.  Respect the user's OpenMP limit while
    53	    // avoiding oversubscription on machines with large default teams.
    54	    constexpr std::ptrdiff_t MIN_BODIES_PER_THREAD = 64;
    55	    const std::ptrdiff_t usefulThreads =
    56	        n > 0 ? (n + MIN_BODIES_PER_THREAD - 1) / MIN_BODIES_PER_THREAD : 1;
    57	    const int maxThreads = omp_get_max_threads();
    58	    const int teamSize = usefulThreads < maxThreads
    59	        ? static_cast<int>(usefulThreads)
    60	        : maxThreads;
    61	
    62	    // Keep a single team alive for all steps.  Both implicit barriers are
    63	    // required: all force reads must precede integration, and every position
    64	    // update must complete before the next step begins.
    65	#pragma omp parallel num_threads(teamSize) default(none) \
    66	    shared(data) firstprivate(n, numSteps)
    67	    {
    68	        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
    69	#pragma omp for schedule(static)
    70	            for (std::ptrdiff_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
    71	                const double x = data[i].pos.x;
    72	                const double y = data[i].pos.y;
    73	                const double z = data[i].pos.z;
    74	                double forceX = 0.0;
    75	                double forceY = 0.0;
    76	                double forceZ = 0.0;
    77	
    78	                for (std::ptrdiff_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
    79	                    const double dx = data[j].pos.x - x;
    80	                    const double dy = data[j].pos.y - y;
    81	                    const double dz = data[j].pos.z - z;
    82	                    const double distSqr =
    83	                        dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
    84	                    const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
    85	                    const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
    86	
    87	                    forceX += dx * invDist3;
    88	                    forceY += dy * invDist3;
    89	                    forceZ += dz * invDist3;
    90	                }
    91

    93	                data[i].vel.y += DT * forceY;
    94	                data[i].vel.z += DT * forceZ;
    95	            }

    99	                data[i].pos.x += data[i].vel.x * DT;

   106	
   107	double computeTotalEnergy(const Bodies& bodies) {
   108	    double energy = 0.0;
   109	    const size_t n = bodies.size();
   110	    
   111	    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
   112	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   113	        energy += 0.5 * (bodies.items[i].vel.x * bodies.items[i].vel.x +
   114	                        bodies.items[i].vel.y * bodies.items[i].vel.y +
   115	                        bodies.items[i].vel.z * bodies.items[i].vel.z);
   116	    }
   117	    
   118	    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
   119	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   120	        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
   121	            const double dx = bodies.items[j].pos.x - bodies.items[i].pos.x;
   122	            const double dy = bodies.items[j].pos.y - bodies.items[i].pos.y;
   123	            const double dz = bodies.items[j].pos.z - bodies.items[i].pos.z;
   124	            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
   125	            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
   126	        }
   127	    }
   128	    
   129	    return energy;
   130	}
   131	
   132	// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
   133	bool validateSimulation(const Bodies& bodies) {
   134	    for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {
   135	        // Check for NaN or Inf values
   136	        if (!std::isfinite(bodies.items[i].pos.x) ||
   137	            !std::isfinite(bodies.items[i].pos.y) ||
   138	            !std::isfinite(bodies.items[i].pos.z) ||
   139	            !std::isfinite(bodies.items[i].vel.x) ||
   140	            !std::isfinite(bodies.items[i].vel.y) ||
   141	            !std::isfinite(bodies.items[i].vel.z)) {
   142	            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
   143	            return false;
   144	        }
   145	        
   146	        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
   147	        const double maxPos = 1e6;
   148	        const double maxVel = 1e6;
   149	        if (std::abs(bodies.items[i].pos.x) > maxPos ||
   150	            std::abs(bodies.items[i].pos.y) > maxPos ||
   151	            std::abs(bodies.items[i].pos.z) > maxPos) {
   152	            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
   153	            return false;
   154	        }
   155	        if (std::abs(bodies.items[i].vel.x) > maxVel ||
   156	            std::abs(bodies.items[i].vel.y) > maxVel ||
   157	            std::abs(bodies.items[i].vel.z) > maxVel) {
   158	            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
   159	            return false;
   160	        }
   161	    }
   162	    return true;
   163	}

   165	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   166	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   167	    printf("Options:\n");
   168	    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
   169	    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
   170	    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
   171	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   172	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   173	}
   174	
   175	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   176	    int numBodies = 1024;
   177	    int numSteps = 10;
   178	    bool validate = false;
   179	    bool printResults = false;
   180	    
   181	    // Parse command line arguments
   182	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   183	        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   184	            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
   185	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   186	            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
   187	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   188	            validate = true;
   189	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   190	            printResults = true;
   220	    // Print results for external validation
   221	    if (printResults) {
   222	        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
   223	        std::vector<double> bodyData;
   224	        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
   225	        for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {
   226	            bodyData.push_back(bodies.items[i].pos.x);
   227	            bodyData.push_back(bodies.items[i].pos.y);
   228	            bodyData.push_back(bodies.items[i].pos.z);
   229	            bodyData.push_back(bodies.items[i].vel.x);
   230	            bodyData.push_back(bodies.items[i].vel.y);
   231	            bodyData.push_back(bodies.items[i].vel.z);
   232	        }
   233	        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
   234	    }
   235	    
   236	    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
   237	    if (validate) {
   238	        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
   239	        
   240	        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
   241	            // Report final energy for reference
   242	            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
   243	            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
   244	            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   245	            return 0;
   246	        } else {
   247	            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   248	            return 1;
   249	        }
   250	    }
   251	    
   252	    return 0;
   253	}

+void randomizeBodies(Bodies& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {

-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+void simulateBodies(Bodies& bodies, const int numSteps) {
+    const std::ptrdiff_t n = static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(bodies.size());
+    Body* const data = bodies.items.data();
+
+    // Very small chunks lose more time at the two step barriers than they
+    // gain from another worker.  Respect the user's OpenMP limit while
+    // avoiding oversubscription on machines with large default teams.
+    constexpr std::ptrdiff_t MIN_BODIES_PER_THREAD = 64;
+    const std::ptrdiff_t usefulThreads =
+        n > 0 ? (n + MIN_BODIES_PER_THREAD - 1) / MIN_BODIES_PER_THREAD : 1;
+    const int maxThreads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    const int teamSize = usefulThreads < maxThreads
+        ? static_cast<int>(usefulThreads)
+        : maxThreads;
+
+    // Keep a single team alive for all steps.  Both implicit barriers are
+    // required: all force reads must precede integration, and every position
+    // update must complete before the next step begins.
+#pragma omp parallel num_threads(teamSize) default(none) \
+    shared(data) firstprivate(n, numSteps)
+    {
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (std::ptrdiff_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+                const double x = data[i].pos.x;
+                const double y = data[i].pos.y;
+                const double z = data[i].pos.z;
+                double forceX = 0.0;
+                double forceY = 0.0;
+                double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+                for (std::ptrdiff_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+                    const double dx = data[j].pos.x - x;
+                    const double dy = data[j].pos.y - y;
+                    const double dz = data[j].pos.z - z;
+                    const double distSqr =
+                        dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                    const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                    const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                    forceX += dx * invDist3;
+                    forceY += dy * invDist3;
+                    forceZ += dz * invDist3;
+                }
+
+                data[i].vel.x += DT * forceX;
+                data[i].vel.y += DT * forceY;
+                data[i].vel.z += DT * forceZ;
+            }
+
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (std::ptrdiff_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+                data[i].pos.x += data[i].vel.x * DT;
+                data[i].pos.y += data[i].vel.y * DT;
+                data[i].pos.z += data[i].vel.z * DT;
+            }
+        }
     }
 }

+void randomizeBodies(Bodies& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {
+        // Retain the original per-body random-number ordering.
+        bodies.items[i].pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;

-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+void simulateBodies(Bodies& bodies, const int numSteps) {
+    const std::ptrdiff_t n = static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(bodies.size());
+    Body* const data = bodies.items.data();
+
+    // Very small chunks lose more time at the two step barriers than they
+    // gain from another worker.  Respect the user's OpenMP limit while
+    // avoiding oversubscription on machines with large default teams.
+    constexpr std::ptrdiff_t MIN_BODIES_PER_THREAD = 64;
+    const std::ptrdiff_t usefulThreads =
+        n > 0 ? (n + MIN_BODIES_PER_THREAD - 1) / MIN_BODIES_PER_THREAD : 1;
+    const int maxThreads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    const int teamSize = usefulThreads < maxThreads
+        ? static_cast<int>(usefulThreads)
+        : maxThreads;
+
+    // Keep a single team alive for all steps.  Both implicit barriers are
+    // required: all force reads must precede integration, and every position
+    // update must complete before the next step begins.
+#pragma omp parallel num_threads(teamSize) default(none) \
+    shared(data) firstprivate(n, numSteps)
+    {
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (std::ptrdiff_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+                const double x = data[i].pos.x;
+                const double y = data[i].pos.y;
+                const double z = data[i].pos.z;
+                double forceX = 0.0;
+                double forceY = 0.0;
+                double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+                for (std::ptrdiff_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+                    const double dx = data[j].pos.x - x;
+                    const double dy = data[j].pos.y - y;
+                    const double dz = data[j].pos.z - z;
+                    const double distSqr =
+                        dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                    const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                    const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                    forceX += dx * invDist3;
+                    forceY += dy * invDist3;
+                    forceZ += dz * invDist3;
+                }
+
+                data[i].vel.x += DT * forceX;
+                data[i].vel.y += DT * forceY;
+                data[i].vel.z += DT * forceZ;
+            }
+
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (std::ptrdiff_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+                data[i].pos.x += data[i].vel.x * DT;
+                data[i].pos.y += data[i].vel.y * DT;
+                data[i].pos.z += data[i].vel.z * DT;
+            }
+        }
     }
 }
